It&apos;s not something a friend should be prying into... right?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She showed a slightly lonely expression but quickly returned to her usual self.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Eventually, we arrived in front of the dorms, keeping a slight distance between us.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;ll be waiting for Kanzaki-kun and the others&apos; reply, okay?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Yeah&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">After watching her back as she hurried into the dorm ahead of me, I lifted my gaze slightly and stared at the sky, stained by the sunset.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The joy of learning a new emotion.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">And what kind of effect that emotion would have on myself, and on others.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Would what came next be prosperity, or ruin?</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Or perhaps, something else entirely?</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I was, at that moment, living a very fulfilling&#x2014;contented moment.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">For that, I had to be truly, deeply grateful.</p><h2 class="heading_2">Part 3</h2><hr/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">A LITTLE PAST 4:30 p.m. on Thursday, around the time Ayanokouji met up with Ichinose and the others.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Having received a message from a certain individual, Shiraishi appeared at the restaurant alone.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">At that hour, most students gathered at the cafe, so the restaurant, where meals were the main focus, was comparatively less busy, with empty seats always standing out.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She sat alone at a table in the back and ordered a cup of black tea from her tablet.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The flavor was not something one could praise, even out of politeness; it tasted like it&apos;d been brewed from a cheap teabag.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Shiraishi imagined that if it were of the same quality as the cafe, the place would have been bustling at this hour as well. Still, it wasn&apos;t that there were no customers at all, just a sparse few. Then, just as her tea was delivered, another student came in and sat down at the table next to Shiraishi&apos;s.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">From over her shoulder, she could hear the electronic chime of a tablet being operated and an order being placed.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Have I kept you waiting?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The charming words came from behind her. A voice, clear and unadulterated, unpolished yet powerful, reached Shiraishi&apos;s ears.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Not at all. I only just arrived myself.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Are you really sure this was the right place to meet?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Yes. It&apos;s easy for our voices to carry even when we&apos;re back-to-back with the sofa in between, don&apos;t you think?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;It&apos;s true that it&apos;s easier to talk since there are few customers, but on the flip side, that means we inevitably stand out. Anyone who enters can spot us easily, and it&apos;s unavoidable that we&apos;ll leave an impression.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">There was no panic in her voice, but she gave off a strong sense of caution.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The situation, where she could only hear her companion&apos;s voice, worked to Shiraishi&apos;s advantage.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">There was no way for the person in question to know that she&apos;d been placed in such a unique environment.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Visually speaking, that may be so. However, it&apos;s impossible for anyone to imagine our relationship from this back-to-back situation. The fact that we might catch someone&apos;s eye also means that they catch ours. As long as we remain calm, there won&apos;t be any problems.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">They&apos;d simply come to the unpopular restaurant individually. Seeing two students from different classes&#x2014;no, different school years&#x2014;the number of people who could imagine they arranged to meet and were conversing on purpose was extremely limited.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I think an ordinary student would just assume we&apos;re here waiting for two different people. However, there are a few individuals I need to be wary of. If one of them, Ayanokouji-senpai, were to see this...&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Even if he didn&apos;t understand it just from witnessing the situation, he would still unconsciously commit this pairing to memory and likely retrieve it later as needed.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">On that day, at that time, Shiraishi and Nanase sat back-to-back in the restaurant. Had they been pretending to be strangers while having a conversation they didn&apos;t want overheard? He would begin a line of deduction an ordinary person could never reach.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If you understood that, may I ask why you still took that risk?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;The risk you&apos;re concerned about, Nanase-san, has already been eliminated. Ayanokouji-kun just entered the gym with Ichinose-san. At the very least, he won&apos;t be out for another hour.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Surely you didn&apos;t ask Ayanokouji-senpai directly, or have someone else find out for you, did you? Even that would be cause for suspicion.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Her tone of voice remained the same, but Shiraishi didn&apos;t miss the faint anxiety it contained.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Don&apos;t worry. The only words we exchanged today were morning greetings.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Then how did you ascertain Ayanokouji-senpai&apos;s location?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;How I procured that information is a trade secret.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She used a line she heard from Ayanokouji himself during a previous exchange.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Forgive my rudeness, but if you used your friend Nishikawa-senpai, I believe she would be ill-suited for the task. There&apos;s no way Ayanokouji-senpai wouldn&apos;t notice if she carelessly tailed him. It&apos;s entirely plausible he would suspect it was your doing, Shiraishi-senpai, since you two are close. Or does she perhaps possess some hidden talent?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">There was a subtle shift in Nanase&apos;s speech that an ordinary person wouldn&apos;t have noticed.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Only when she spoke the name Nishikawa was there a truly faint, yet deliberate, emphasis.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You&apos;re aware that she isn&apos;t capable of handling such a major role, aren&apos;t you? My apologies, but Nishikawa-san is merely a friend. Your attempt to read so deeply into things is meaningless.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...Is that so?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;It&apos;s all well and good to meticulously check for any missteps on my part, but you&apos;ve exposed your own foolishness quite spectacularly, Nanase-san. To let an uninvolved student discover the existence of your second phone and then to be overheard speaking with someone who was supposed to have left the school.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You&apos;re right. I was careless in that regard.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;ve taken measures to sufficiently silence Hashimoto-kun and Morishita-san, who happened to witness it. It&apos;s possible it may eventually reach Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s ears, but for the time being, it should be fine.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;So you took care of it for me. Thank you very much.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Shiraishi picked up the saucer with her left hand, then placed the fingers of her right hand on the handle of the cup.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Shall we get to the point now? Contacting me is a clear violation of the rules.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I am fully aware that up until now, you&apos;ve lived your life at this school as a completely ordinary student. However&#x2014;I, too, have a job that only I can do, so this was unavoidable. Please, I need your help.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;What is it you want me to do?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I want to get Ayanokouji-senpai expelled. As soon as possible, and at a time he can&apos;t predict.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Closing her eyes, Shiraishi mulled over Nanase&apos;s words, letting them sink in deep.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was a tone of impatience, not one of deceit. She assessed its credibility within herself.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I sympathize with your feelings, but I must decline to offer my assistance.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Why...is that?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That&apos;s because I am, strictly speaking, an observer. From the very beginning, I was never given any role beyond that. It is precisely because I didn&apos;t get involved in anything for these past two years that, from Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s perspective, I was nothing more than &apos;Female Student A&apos; from another class&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Then why have you started to close the distance now? Even if he doesn&apos;t know who you are, Shiraishi-senpai, he might recognize that you&apos;re not an ordinary student.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I suppose so. But it can&apos;t be helped, can it? Through an ironic twist of fate, he and I ended up in the same class. By chance, we ended up in adjacent seats. And by chance, an opportunity arose for the two of us to speak in the classroom one morning... Given all that, there&apos;s no way I can possibly suppress this impulse...&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Shiraishi smiled as she recalled that event, the morning they&apos;d spoken alone in the classroom.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I will spend my days with Ayanokouji-kun as a classmate. For now, I just want to purely enjoy this environment. Nothing more, nothing less, simply as an observer. Besides, that nuisance, Sakayanagi-san, has voluntarily withdrawn from the school.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Is that so... I understand. It seems it will be difficult to rely on you as things stand, Shiraishi-senpai.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">To take a breath, Nanase took a sip of the freshly delivered iced milk through a straw.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Have your fellow students also been given a similar role, Shiraishi-senpai? That is, the role of an observer?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Who knows. The possibility that they&apos;ve been given the same role as me isn&apos;t zero, but I don&apos;t know. All that is required of us is to spend three years as ordinary students. It&apos;s as simple as that.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I will take action while Ayanokouji-senpai is still within reach. If I cannot receive your cooperation, Shiraishi-senpai, then I may have to find one of your fellow students who will cooperate.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Even if you threaten me, cooperation is a difficult matter.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I am from that side.&quot;<sup id="fnref-1-87c39c1f-e305-518e-87f6-94b3a5050825"><a class="footnote-ref" href="#fn-1-87c39c1f-e305-518e-87f6-94b3a5050825" data-ref="1" epub:type="noteref">[1]</a></sup></p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Aren&apos;t you leaving a little something out? &apos;Supposedly,&apos; isn&apos;t it?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...My apologies. It is true that my position may be rather ambiguous. However, isn&apos;t it precisely because my position is different that I&apos;m not in violation of the rules?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That&apos;s true. The reason I accepted your invitation today, Nanase-san, was because I wanted to reassure you regarding the matter with Hashimoto-kun and Morishita-san. I have no intention of cooperating in any other matter.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;At least give me some information... Please, just disclose the names of your associates. After that, we&apos;ll handle&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If you wish to know, I suggest you ask your superiors directly. Tsukishiro-san, was it? You have someone reliable you can turn to, don&apos;t you? Not that I&apos;m fond of him.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Shiraishi placed her finished cup on its saucer and rose from the sofa.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I understand that you will not be cooperating for the time being. However, there is just one thing I&apos;d like you to remember. May I state it?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;What is it?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That if I am expelled before Ayanokouji-senpai&apos;s fate is decided... things may very well head in a direction that Shirogane-sensei would not desire&#x2014;I ask that you please bear this in mind.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Very well. I shall keep it in mind.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Shiraishi answered as such without so much as a nod, and left the restaurant before Nanase.</p><hr class="footnote-rule"/><ol class="footnotes"><li data-id="87c39c1f-e305-518e-87f6-94b3a5050825" data-ref="1" class="footnote first-footnote" id="fn-1-87c39c1f-e305-518e-87f6-94b3a5050825"><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left"><a class="footnote-backref" href="#fnref-1-87c39c1f-e305-518e-87f6-94b3a5050825">[1]: </a>Nanaese&apos;s statement &quot;I am from that side&quot; (&#x305d;&#x3061;&#x3089;&#x5074;&#x306e;&#x4eba;&#x9593;&#x3067;&#x3059;) is her saying she&apos;s aligned with the anti-Ayanokoji faction (&quot;superiors&quot;, Tsukishiro etc.) Shiraishi responds with &#x4e00;&#x5fdc; which means &quot;for the time being&quot;, &quot;tentatively&quot;, or &quot;In principle&quot; she is essentially saying &quot;Your position isn&apos;t as strong as you&apos;re making it sound&quot;, she dismisses Nanase&apos;s declaration.</p></li></ol><h1 class="heading_1"><a class="heading-link" href="/read/ss-y3-v2/2">Epilogue:<br/>Another Story Begins</a></h1><hr/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left"><em>IT WAS JUST before 11:00 a.m. on a Saturday in late May</em>&#xa0;when I left my room for a meeting.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">And just as I left the dorm lobby, the person I was there to meet called out to me.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Good morning, Horikita-san.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Karuizawa-san, who seemed to have arrived a little earlier than planned, was smiling.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Good morning. I apologize for contacting you so suddenly last night.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&#xa0;As I&#x2019;d just mentioned, I had sent the message asking to see Karuizawa-san last night. It wouldn&apos;t have been surprising if she had plans, given how many friends she had, but she readily agreed to meet.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;It&apos;s totally fine! I mean, getting an invitation from you, Horikita-san, was kinda new and exciting, so I was happy.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She was looking forward to it, she added with a smile.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;But...why are you in your uniform?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I have a Student Council&#xa0;matter I need to attend to, at two o&apos;clock.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Entering school grounds in casual clothes was prohibited. I explained that it would be a hassle to go out before noon in casual wear, only to have to return to change into my uniform. On days off, some students came to school for club activities, so even though we were a minority, I wasn&apos;t worried that I would be the only one standing out.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Being student council president sounds like a lot of work. I could never do something like that.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She said, &quot;No way, no way,&quot; while gazing toward Keyaki Mall.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;So, what&apos;s the plan? I&apos;m actually kinda looking forward to you escorting me around, Horikita-san.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If that&apos;s what you were expecting, then I&apos;m afraid I&apos;ll have to apologize. I didn&apos;t invite you to hang out for fun.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As I offered my light apology, Karuizawa-san&apos;s eyes widened, as if she&apos;d just realized something.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Eh, ah... Don&apos;t tell me... you have something you want to know about Ayanokouji-kun?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You&apos;re sharp. That is one of my objectives.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Well, the special exam just ended smoothly and all, so I kinda figured that might be it. But even so, there&apos;s no guarantee I can actually help, you know?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I understand that. But if it&apos;s something even you don&apos;t know, then I can give up.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">After all, among everyone I knew, she was without a doubt the one who knew Ayanokouji-kun best.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Okay. Then ask me anything. If it&apos;s something I know, I&apos;ll answer whatever!&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">After saying that, she seemed to have second thoughts, scratching her head in a fluster.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Sorry, maybe &apos;anything&apos; is a bit much. But I can talk about most things, so, yeah.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Karuizawa-san corrected herself, her face turning red.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was a slightly curious reaction, but it would be more than enough if she simply told me what she could.</p><img src="/hola-lector-agua/images/books/ss-y3-v2/OEBPS/xFbJRJTHEXJEPINZREygZ.jpg" loading="lazy" decoding="async" alt="image"/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Thank you.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Karuizawa-san&apos;s devoted&#x2014;no, positive response put my heart at ease.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I have no intention of keeping secrets or telling you any clumsy lies, either. I&apos;ll tell you everything about why I want to know about Ayanokouji-kun and what my thoughts are, so I&apos;d like you to hear me out.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Of course. I might not look it, but I&apos;m great at keeping secrets, so bring it on!&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Trusting her, I told her that as a first step toward my goal of defeating Ayanokouji-kun, I planned to start by learning more about him, mentioning Kushida-san and Ibuki-san&apos;s names as well.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">And that to do so, I was also thinking of looking into his roots.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I explained all of this to Karuizawa-san.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I see. I... Yeah, since before I started dating Kiyotaka, ah&#x2014;I mean, Ayanokouji-kun, I used to wonder a lot about things like what he was like in middle school, so I get it. Oh, and sorry, I might still slip up and call him by his first name.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I don&apos;t mind that at all. If it&apos;s difficult for you to use his surname, you&apos;re welcome to do otherwise.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Nn-nn, this is about setting boundaries. For me... you know?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I see&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I was dating Ayanokouji-kun, but to be honest, I doubt I have much more information than you do. I tried asking him about his past a few times, but I don&apos;t think he ever really gave me a straight answer.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Things like which prefecture he lived in, or where his middle school was?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Yeah. I asked about things like what foods he likes and dislikes, or his taste in clothes, but&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As she made a gesture of recollection, Karuizawa-san began telling me what she saw and heard for herself.</p><h2 class="heading_2">Part 1</h2><hr/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">WE ARRIVED AT Keyaki Mall. Continuing our conversation, we strolled idly through the mall. It was just as I was thinking of suggesting we get lunch in a little while.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Actually, there&apos;s one more reason I called you here. And I was lucky enough to find her.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Huh? Whaddya mean?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">To the head-tilting Karuizawa-san, I simply directed my gaze in that direction and had her follow.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...Amasawa-san?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She muttered the name upon spotting the back of a lone student with red twin-tails walking ahead of us.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Amasawa Ichika-san has a connection to Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s past. That&apos;s why I&apos;m investigating her right now.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Ahh... So that&apos;s how it is.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Next to me, Karuizawa-san showed no surprise, but rather a look of understanding.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Did you perhaps know about their relationship?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Nah, not at all. But I&apos;ve seen Ayanokouji-kun and Amasawa-san talking a little bit. I kinda had a feeling it wasn&apos;t just some simple senpai-and-kouhai relationship they made at this school.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It didn&apos;t seem like there was a difference in the information we had, but her opinion was reassuring.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The fact that we sensed the same thing gave me encouragement to continue with the tedious investigation.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Are we gonna tail her now? Hey, should we?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...You seem awfully excited about this.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I don&apos;t mind doing spy-like stuff. I mean, is there anyone who actually dislikes it?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Well, if I thought of it as an extension of a game, perhaps it wouldn&apos;t be so bad...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Our target likely had a connection to Ayanokouji-kun and moreover, she was considerably skilled.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Knowing that there was a high probability of losing our only lead if we failed, I found it impossible to enjoy.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;For now, I&apos;ll tell you what I&apos;ve gleaned from observing Amasawa-san lately.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was something I&apos;d noticed from watching Amasawa-san after school over the past few days.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">She usually acted alone most of the time, and I never saw her spending time with classmates or friends. Of course, she would respond with a smile if spoken to by anyone, boy or girl, but she would immediately return to being alone and would never join the group that had approached her.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It appeared to be her stance not to form any particularly close relationships.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">That preference for solitude seemed somewhat similar to Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s, but perhaps that was a stretch. In his case, it wasn&apos;t that he didn&apos;t make friends; if anything, he was the type who couldn&apos;t, even if he wanted to.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That was an act... No, it couldn&apos;t have been, could it? Surely not.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I don&apos;t think that was an act. That similarity seems like another thing they have in common.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Setting aside how pathetic it was that I hadn&apos;t known that about him, his way of interacting with people really wasn&apos;t very skillful. That was probably his true self.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Time passed as we continued to follow Amasawa-san&apos;s back from a distance.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Ideally, I should&apos;ve spoken with her directly.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">But the world wasn&apos;t so kind that she would talk honestly just like that.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Another person, this time a male student, called out to Amasawa-san.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">They only spoke for about five seconds and it appeared to be little more than a greeting.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Even so, I made a point to remember every student who was on speaking terms with her.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Gathering information indirectly was the only thing I could do at the moment.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Just as I was memorizing his face, the phone I held in my right hand vibrated.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;What&apos;s up?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Hold on.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left"><em>[&apos;I&apos;m waiting in the Student Council room.&apos;]</em></p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I took out my phone, looked at the text on the screen and tilted my head in my mind.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...Who could this be?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Such a short message, from an unregistered contact.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">There was no date or time specified, but did that mean right now...?</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">If my two o&apos;clock appointment today had been moved up, it wouldn&apos;t have been strange to get word from Nanase-san. Was this something else, then? Even as I was lost in thought, Amasawa-san continued to walk through Keyaki Mall.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Though I knew I had to follow her, I blanked my phone&apos;s screen and closed my eyes.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I suppose it can&apos;t be helped...&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As long as I&apos;d been entrusted with the position of student council president, I couldn&apos;t just act on personal feelings alone.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">If someone was waiting in the student council room, I had a duty to respond as best as I could.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Or, even if there was a 99% chance it wasn&apos;t, it might have been from Ayanokouji-kun.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As a realistic possibility, it might also have been part of the next special exam.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;m sorry. I know it&apos;s sudden, but I have to go to the student council.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I apologized for having to cut things short before we could even have lunch, despite being the one who invited her.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Don&apos;t worry about it at all. And hey, I won&apos;t go tailing Amasawa-san on my own, so don&apos;t you worry.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Realizing on her own what I was about to warn her against, Karuizawa-san replied accordingly.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I apologized to her one more time and then decided to head for school.</p><h2 class="heading_2">Part 2</h2><hr/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">AFTER ABOUT TEN minutes, I arrived in front of the student council room, but no one was there.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">No new messages had arrived on my phone either.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">On a holiday at this hour, the unused student council room was locked, as one would expect.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...A prank?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Though I was suspicious, I used my key to enter the student council room just in case.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Of course, no one was in the silent room and not a single sound could be heard. After returning to the hallway, I decided to wait there for the person who sent the message, just in case.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">But, as I expected, the person I was waiting for never appeared, and time simply passed by.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I wonder if it would&apos;ve been better to follow Amasawa-san after all. A hint of that regret began to sprout, but since I still had about two hours before my official student council duties, I decided to head back for the time being.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Afterward, I went down the stairs to the first floor. From there, I started walking toward the entrance.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Student Council President Horikita.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Just as I took a few steps down the hallway, a student suddenly called my name.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">But no one was in my line of sight until now. Surprised, I turned to look behind me.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You&apos;re... Ishigami-kun, right? Do you need something from me?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Ishigami Kyou-kun from Class 2-A stood there, dressed in his school uniform.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As I recalled his name, I wondered why such an unusual person would approach me.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was Saturday, after all. The chances of running into anyone other than students with club activities should&apos;ve been extremely low.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;It&apos;s been a while. I have something I&apos;d like to discuss, so may I have a moment of your time?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Of course. It&apos;s been quite a while since we&apos;ve spoken like this.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That time, it was you who approached me, President.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That&apos;s right.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I recalled the time I invited Ishigami-kun to join the student council. He turned me down flat, but whether or not someone joined was a personal matter, so naturally, I couldn&apos;t blame him.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I was pretty sure Ayanokouji-kun was with me then, too...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Would you mind if we moved somewhere else?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Somewhere else? Is it something... difficult to talk about?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;d prefer if no one saw us. I don&apos;t intend to take up much of your time.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">He said that and without waiting for my permission, turned his back and started walking.</p><img src="/hola-lector-agua/images/books/ss-y3-v2/OEBPS/IeHzmpOSNxNIYeShsIchb.jpg" loading="lazy" decoding="async" alt="image"/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I didn&apos;t have any pressing reason to hurry back, so I didn&apos;t mind going along with him, but...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">There were no other people wandering around the school on this day off, so it seemed like we could talk anywhere without issue. I wondered if that meant it was something he really didn&apos;t want anyone to overhear...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Were you the one who sent me the message?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;What makes you think so?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;So you&apos;re not going to start with denial.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">When I mentioned the message, he didn&apos;t seem puzzled and appeared to know what I was talking about.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Furthermore, his attitude seemed to be a natural admission and it didn&apos;t look like he had any intention of hiding it.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If you have something to discuss, on a holiday like today, I can listen to you anywhere. But you specifically said you didn&apos;t want to be seen. If we used the student council room, someone might come by, or I might have to call the other student council members. Plus, you approached me on my way back from the student council room. That way, you could confirm I was acting alone before making contact.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I figured he must have stayed out of my sight, waiting for the opportune moment.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I admit it. I apologize for the roundabout method.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I don&apos;t particularly mind, but if this is a student council consultation requiring urgency, I may not be able to keep it to myself.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The bigger the problem, the more necessary it became to release the information as the student council.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">An obligation to report not only to the students but also to the teachers would likely arise.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You need not worry on that account.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That isn&apos;t something for you to judge personally, though.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Regarding my consultation&#x2014;it seems you&apos;ve been looking into Amasawa, President.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Ishigami-kun spoke without turning to face me, continuing to walk at a steady pace.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...What do you mean?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">While I felt a slight sense of unease at his unexpected words, I first feigned ignorance.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I&apos;d never made a connection between Ishigami-kun and Amasawa-san, but perhaps my assessment of him had been too naive. She must have noticed I was tailing her early on and requested Ishigami-kun&apos;s cooperation. That was a perfectly plausible scenario, and yet I hadn&apos;t considered it.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You&apos;re not going to start with denial, are you?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">He stopped and said so as he turned back, his gaze meeting mine without hesitation.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was to expose and assess the agitation I was trying to hide.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">My belated wariness toward him spiked all at once.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Amasawa and I are in the same class. If she makes any suspicious moves, it&apos;s only natural that I&apos;d be informed.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...I suppose so.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">For the last few days, I&apos;d been under the self-assessed assumption that Amasawa-san was a student who preferred to act alone.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">But it seemed that wasn&apos;t actually the case.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Did she tell Ishigami-kun that she was troubled by me following her and asked for his help&#x2014;?</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">No, but...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;So you&apos;re displeased that your classmate is being investigated. Is this a warning?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;No. However, I need to know why the student council is investigating Amasawa. If she is the source of some problem, we as a class will be forced to respond, you see.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was a perfectly logical reason. For Class A, which appeared to be managed with stability, it was only right to be sensitive to a classmate&apos;s misconduct and it was no wonder they had their feelers out.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;In that case, you can rest assured. She isn&apos;t in any kind of trouble.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">First, I had to clear up Ishigami-kun&apos;s misunderstanding.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">And doing so would also naturally lead to my own objective.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Would you mind telling me the reason?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Do you know of a student from third-year Class C named Ayanokouji-kun?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;ve never spoken to him directly, but he&apos;s been the subject of various discussions among the second-years as the student who voluntarily transferred from Class A to Class C.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I see. So you&apos;re aware of him, as I&apos;d expect. When I bring this sort of thing up, most students want to ask me how I feel about it, but you don&apos;t seem to be that way.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Unfortunately, I have no interest in students from other years. With that said, I fail to see what this has to do with you investigating Amasawa.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It seemed Ishigami-kun was, through and through, only interested in his own class&apos;s affairs.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">That was hardly good news for me... but I continued my explanation anyway.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;m searching for any student who might know about his life before he enrolled at this school. And in doing so, the possibility arose that Amasawa-san is an old acquaintance of Ayanokouji-kun. That&apos;s why I want to ask her for details, but she and I aren&apos;t particularly close. I&apos;m hesitant to ask her directly, so I was in the middle of trying to find some kind of lead.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Why is the student council president investigating the one who betrayed her class?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;While it isn&apos;t a frequent occurrence at this school, now that a class transfer happened, it must be dealt with. He has now moved to Class C and we&apos;re now rivals. Surely it isn&apos;t so strange for me to try and gather as much information as I can in order to defeat him in the future, right?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;To know your enemy, you must get inside their camp. Is that what you&apos;re saying?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Something like that. I may continue to investigate Amasawa-san, but rest assured, it won&apos;t have any negative impact on the second-year Class A.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I understand. However, there&apos;s no telling how others will perceive this. If the student council president has her eyes on Amasawa, it could create the misunderstanding that our class has a problem. I would like you to resolve this as quickly as possible.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It wasn&apos;t as if I wanted to take my time either, but if it were that simple, I wouldn&apos;t be having such a hard time.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;For now, I don&apos;t have any information regarding Ayanokouji-senpai, but I have someone in mind who might know about his past.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Really? Who is it?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I asked in response to his intriguing statement, but Ishigami-kun didn&apos;t answer immediately.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I don&apos;t mind telling you, but I need you to promise me one thing. You must absolutely keep the identity of the person who gave you this student&apos;s name&#x2014;in other words, me&#x2014;confidential.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">For a moment, the thought that he might ask for Private Points as an information fee crossed my mind, but it seemed I was mistaken.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If that&apos;s what you wish, I promise to protect your anonymity.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;The person in question is a student with fairly sharp intuition. It&apos;s to be expected that they&apos;ll try to figure out how you found your way to them. You can handle that with pride befitting Class A and the student council president, correct?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">He asked for confirmation, deliberately applying pressure as if to make a point.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Perhaps, from Ishigami-kun&apos;s perspective, this person was just that troublesome to deal with.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">No, that was something I&apos;d known from the very beginning.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">If this was a person who knew Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s past, it stood to reason that they would be someone of considerable ability.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;ll do my best. For now, all I can do is ask you to trust my resolve.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I answered him that way, fully intending to refuse if he asked me to wager something or enter a contract.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Very well. I will place my trust in you, Student Council President Horikita and provide the information.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...Thank you.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;She&apos;s a female student from second-year Class D named Nanase. As she&apos;s a member of the student council, you must know her well.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The name of a person I hadn&apos;t considered a possibility, someone who was far too close.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Upon hearing it, my thoughts came to a complete and sudden halt.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;m afraid that&apos;s not the case. I had the opportunity to have tea with her and asked a similar question. She told me she didn&apos;t know.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Perhaps she simply wasn&apos;t telling the truth?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It wasn&apos;t just a passing thought; Ishigami-kun looked at me, his tone of voice hinting at a sliver of confidence.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...Why do you think that? I can&apos;t doubt her without reason.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I am, after all, a member of Class A, so I&apos;m constantly gathering information on the other classes. And in doing so, I&apos;ve learned that Nanase was in contact with Ayanokouji-senpai from very early on.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That&apos;s&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">It was because Nanase-san participated in the special exam last year, one revealed to only a portion of the first-years, in which they would receive Private Points if they could expel Ayanokouji-kun. I started to say as much but thought better of it. Not all the current second-years knew of that affair, nor had it spread to the third-years.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I decided no one would gain anything if I made a careless remark and dredged up the past.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Last year&apos;s first special exam that you second-years faced. Our class made contact with Nanase-san&apos;s class to coordinate, so perhaps that&apos;s the connection?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;m aware of that as well. But can we dismiss the possibility that Nanase orchestrated even that initial alliance for the sake of contacting Ayanokouji-senpai?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;If they were old friends, she could&apos;ve just spoken to him openly. There would be no need to go to such lengths&#x2014;&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;You may be right. If I&apos;m just overthinking it, that&apos;s fine. Please forget about it.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">At that, Ishigami-kun retracted his statement.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Nanase-san knows about Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s past?</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The only information to go on was Ishigami-kun&apos;s statement, so it wasn&apos;t particularly credible. However, now that such a possibility had been suggested, even for a moment, it was etched into a corner of my mind against my will.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I felt it was impossible, but if it were true&#x2014;that would mean...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;...I wonder, could I ask for your cooperation?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Cooperation? What for?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I want to investigate Nanase-san, just in case. Though you&apos;re in different classes, you&apos;re both second-years, so I&apos;m sure you have some contact in your daily lives.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;That may be true, but I think it would be difficult for me to cooperate. That&apos;s part of the reason I wished to remain anonymous, as it seems Nanase dislikes me.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;She dislikes you...? From the class competition or something?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I&apos;ve had to intervene in our constant troubles with Housen of Class D, even outside of special exams. While I can&apos;t determine the exact reason, I&apos;m certain that she dislikes me.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The OAA confirmed that Ishigami-kun possessed exceptional academic ability. He didn&apos;t seem to be his class&apos;s leader, but assuming he contributed to his class in a position close to that of a strategist, it was a natural dynamic for him to view Nanase-san&#x2014;or rather, her entire class&#x2014;with hostility.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">However... she wasn&apos;t the type to openly dislike someone on a personal level just because they were class rivals. At least, that was the judgment I made based on the information I had at my disposal.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">If Ishigami-kun had no way of knowing that about her, then it was understandable that he&apos;d take it that way. Or perhaps the boy standing before me simply didn&apos;t want to get dragged into trouble and was making an excuse not to cooperate.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I didn&apos;t know if he was someone I could trust, but right now, I just wanted a collaborator.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Very well. I cannot act directly, but I&apos;ll try to do some probing to an extent that my identity isn&apos;t discovered. On the chance that Nanase is unrelated, I&apos;ll also check if Amasawa or anyone else seems to know about Ayanokouji-senpai. Student Council President Horikita, you just have to keep your promise to me and move forward with your own investigation as you please.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Yes, thank you. Even if I&apos;m pressed for information, I&apos;ll protect you at all costs.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;I am hoping for positive results. Well then.&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">With those words, Ishigami-kun turned his back on me and started walking in the opposite direction of the entrance. It was likely a precaution, born from his wariness of being seen with me by Nanase-san.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">The more I tried to investigate Ayanokouji-kun, the more I felt like I was sinking into a swamp.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">&quot;Ayanokouji-kun, just what are you...?&quot;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Even so, I had no choice but to recklessly push through the mud and continue chasing after him.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">However, as I renewed my determination, I was still completely unaware.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Of Ayanokouji-kun&apos;s birth.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Of the existence of an education and a facility so unreal and harsh that no one who lived a normal life could ever imagine it.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Of the fact that he was the only one who survived, as other children broke one after another.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Of the cruel fate that awaited him later in life&#x2014;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">That was surely the turning point.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">My reunion with him, with Ishigami-kun, was the beginning of a great change in my life.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Becoming deeply involved in the life of the man named Ayanokouji Kiyotaka&#x2014;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">An orchestrated encounter.</p><h1 class="heading_1"><a class="heading-link" href="/read/ss-y3-v2/2">Author&apos;s Afterword</a></h1><hr/><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">THE YEAR IS passing by in a flash these days. This is Kinugasa.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I&apos;m getting older, and my hernia is starting to flare up again, so I can&apos;t say I&apos;m in perfect shape, but other than that, I&apos;m actually getting healthier every year.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">My blood pressure and whatnot&#x2014;the numbers that were a little bad have all trended toward improvement and are now at a level appropriate for my age. All that&apos;s left is, really, the hernia... That&apos;s the one thing I can&apos;t seem to do anything about. But I&apos;ll keep at it.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">In the next volume, Volume 3, since we&apos;re heading into early summer a bit ahead of schedule, that annual event will be coming up sooner.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Volume 2 was, if anything, a more relaxed and heartwarming installment, but this next one might have a bit more of a bite to it.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">But since it&apos;s me saying this, you&apos;d best take it with a grain of salt.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">While I feel there&apos;s still a long way to go until the conclusion, when I think about how the first term is already drawing to a close, perhaps it&apos;s going by surprisingly fast. I want to avoid touching too much on the main story or the future in the afterword, so I&apos;ll stop here for now, brief as it is.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Now for a bit of pointless talk about my private life. Feel free to skip this. (Just filling pages.)</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">To put it simply, with games I used to play a lot like Mario Kart or Smash Bros., I&apos;ve had my fill after just twenty or thirty minutes of playing with my kids, and I don&apos;t have a single game app on my phone other than YouMaji. I used to think, &apos;I can&apos;t imagine a time without games, what else is there to do?&apos;... But now, before I know it, the day is just over!</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">And my kids just keep growing up big and healthy in the blink of an eye...</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I&apos;m keenly experiencing Janet&apos;s Principle, sensing that from now on, I&apos;ll probably feel time accelerate more and more as I get older.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">Well then, everyone&#x2014;</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">I look forward to the day we can meet again before the year is out.</p><p class="paragraph" style="text-align: left">As long as there aren&apos;t any major problems, y&apos;know.
